  ret void
}

define dso_local void @_mlir_ciface_custom_mrgsort(ptr noundef %src_proposals, i64 noundef %off0, i64 noundef %off1, i64 noundef %off2, i64 noundef %off3, i32 noundef %len0, i32 noundef %len1, i32 noundef %len2, i32 noundef %len3, i1 noundef zeroext %if_exhausted_suspension, i32 noundef %valid_bit, i32 noundef %repeat_times, ptr noundef %dst) #4 {
entry:
  %src_proposals.addr = alloca ptr, align 8
  %off0.addr = alloca i64, align 8
  %off1.addr = alloca i64, align 8
  %off2.addr = alloca i64, align 8
  %off3.addr = alloca i64, align 8
  %len0.addr = alloca i32, align 4
  %len1.addr = alloca i32, align 4
  %len2.addr = alloca i32, align 4
  %len3.addr = alloca i32, align 4
  %if_exhausted_suspension.addr = alloca i8, align 1
  %valid_bit.addr = alloca i32, align 4
  %repeat_times.addr = alloca i32, align 4
  %dst.addr = alloca ptr, align 8
  %npp = alloca i64, align 8
  %src_ptr = alloca ptr addrspace(6), align 8
  %addr_array = alloca [4 x ptr addrspace(6)], align 8
  %config = alloca i64, align 8
  %lens = alloca i64, align 8
  %dst_ptr = alloca ptr addrspace(6), align 8
  store ptr %src_proposals, ptr %src_proposals.addr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  store i64 %off0, ptr %off0.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  store i64 %off1, ptr %off1.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  store i64 %off2, ptr %off2.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  store i64 %off3, ptr %off3.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  store i32 %len0, ptr %len0.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
  store i32 %len1, ptr %len1.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
  store i32 %len2, ptr %len2.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
  store i32 %len3, ptr %len3.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
  %frombool = zext i1 %if_exhausted_suspension to i8
  store i8 %frombool, ptr %if_exhausted_suspension.addr, align 1, !tbaa !115
  store i32 %valid_bit, ptr %valid_bit.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
  store i32 %repeat_times, ptr %repeat_times.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
  store ptr %dst, ptr %dst.addr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  call void @llvm.lifetime.start.p0(i64 8, ptr %npp) #3
  store i64 2, ptr %npp, align 8, !tbaa !16
  call void @llvm.lifetime.start.p0(i64 8, ptr %src_ptr) #3
  %0 = load ptr, ptr %src_proposals.addr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %aligned = getelementptr inbounds %struct.memref_t, ptr %0, i32 0, i32 1
  %1 = load ptr addrspace(6), ptr %aligned, align 8, !tbaa !38
  %2 = load ptr, ptr %src_proposals.addr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %offset = getelementptr inbounds %struct.memref_t, ptr %2, i32 0, i32 2
  %3 = load i64, ptr %offset, align 8, !tbaa !40
  %add.ptr = getelementptr inbounds float, ptr addrspace(6) %1, i64 %3
  store ptr addrspace(6) %add.ptr, ptr %src_ptr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  call void @llvm.lifetime.start.p0(i64 32, ptr %addr_array) #3
  %4 = load ptr addrspace(6), ptr %src_ptr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %5 = load i64, ptr %off0.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  %mul = mul i64 %5, 2
  %add.ptr1 = getelementptr inbounds float, ptr addrspace(6) %4, i64 %mul
  %arrayidx = getelementptr inbounds [4 x ptr addrspace(6)], ptr %addr_array, i64 0, i64 0
  store ptr addrspace(6) %add.ptr1, ptr %arrayidx, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %6 = load ptr addrspace(6), ptr %src_ptr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %7 = load i64, ptr %off1.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  %mul2 = mul i64 %7, 2

  %arrayidx4 = getelementptr inbounds [4 x ptr addrspace(6)], ptr %addr_array, i64 0, i64 1
  store ptr addrspace(6) %add.ptr3, ptr %arrayidx4, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %8 = load ptr addrspace(6), ptr %src_ptr, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %9 = load i64, ptr %off2.addr, align 8, !tbaa !16
  %mul5 = mul i64 %9, 2
  %add.ptr6 = getelementptr inbounds float, ptr addrspace(6) %8, i64 %mul5
  %arrayidx7 = getelementptr inbounds [4 x ptr addrspace(6)], ptr %addr_array, i64 0, i64 2
  store ptr addrspace(6) %add.ptr6, ptr %arrayidx7, align 8, !tbaa !3
  %10 = load ptr addrspace(6), ptr %src_ptr, align 8, !tbaa !3

  %tobool = trunc i8 %16 to i1

  %conv20 = zext i32 %and19 to i64

  %24 = load i32, ptr %len3.addr, align 4, !tbaa !59
